- aggregate boundary: S-07 owns the crawl-to-ingestion boundary and the durable handoff into parsing. It does not own parsing or indexing execution: WF-006 is S-08's. This row contracts only the obligations CAP-008 places on the crawl side plus the handoff.
- value objects: AttemptNumber, ReplayGeneration, RecoveryOfId, FailedSubset, ReasonCode, CoverageStatus, ReadinessState.
- domain service: Not applicable - CAP-008 names no domain service.
- repository: `CrawlRepository`, `IngestionJobRepository`, `DocumentRepository`; `ParsingJobRepository` and `IndexingJobRepository` read-only at the handoff boundary.
- persistence model: Immutable Crawl, parse and index attempt history. The durable handoff record is the succeeded IngestionJob with its valid `source_document` Evidence: `ingestion-interim-v1` requires that only a succeeded job with valid Evidence enters parsing, so the job record itself is the handoff rather than a separate queue message. Attempt, lease and replay records are implementation-owned technical infrastructure and are not product entities.
- migration: Not applicable at this row - no product schema object is added. The `unique (parsing_job_id)` constraint that would narrow ParsingJob-to-IndexingJob is WITHHELD under OD-027 and MUST NOT be added.
- transaction boundary: The durable handoff commits with the IngestionJob success, so a parsing consumer can never observe a succeeded job without its Evidence. Recovery commands each commit in their own transaction and never transition a terminal Crawl or a succeeded job backward.
- concurrency: Each successful same-version job advances the Document exactly once, per CAP-008 Success Condition. Concurrent completion or replay cannot change an earlier snapshot: the Evaluation Input Snapshot is derived from the complete parse and Evidence manifest and is immutable once taken.
- idempotency: Recovery is idempotent by its idempotency key and expected state version. A duplicate queue delivery re-enters the same job identity and produces no second Document advance, because the same-version advance-exactly-once rule is enforced on the Document rather than on the delivery.
- background job: As MTX-030 for crawl and ingestion. Parsing and indexing jobs are WF-006 work owned by S-08.
- queue: As MTX-030.
- retry policy: Only the bounded WF-005, `parsing-interim-v1` or `indexing-interim-v1` retry and authorized recovery contracts apply, per CAP-008 Product Behavior. No hidden retry exists: CAP-008's Non-goal is automatic hidden retries without audit trace.
- terminal failure: Exhausted or nonretryable parse or indexing, blocked readiness, missing external input, or denied recovery creates the enumerated state, reason, subset and events without hidden retry, dropped manifest member, stale Evaluation mutation, or unclassified persistence.
- reconciliation: Every admitted Crawl and parse member and every created IndexingJob has an exact terminal or scheduled outcome. Readiness -- ready-full, ready-partial or blocked -- derives from the complete parse and Evidence manifest without waiting for the Retrieval projection, so a lagging projection never blocks or falsifies readiness.
- domain events: Crawl, ingestion, parsing attempt and replay, failed-subset, readiness, Evaluation-transition and correlation telemetry per CAP-008. The crawl-side events are the MTX-030 set; parsing and indexing events belong to S-08.
- event payload: As MTX-030, plus the exact failed subset, reason codes and attempt and replay counts.
- event producer: `Workflows::Wf005` for the crawl and ingestion limbs; `Workflows::Wf006` for the parsing and indexing limbs, owned by S-08.
- event consumers: Parsing consumes the durable handoff, not an event. S-16 read models consume progress state.
- serializer: Not applicable - no transport exposure.
- authorization entry point: The recovery command, before mutation. A SecurityOperator reaches it only through an active Support Session naming the target and action.
- permission checks: `crawl.recover` and `ingestion.recover`, per CAP-008 Security Implications that recovery actions require authorized roles. A SecurityOperator has no standing recovery authority; the Support Session is the grant.
- tenant boundary: As MTX-030.
- error contract: As MTX-030 for the crawl limb. Recovery denial produces the enumerated state and reason with no side effect, per PRULE-022: an unauthorized, stale or altered replay MUST have no side effect.
- audit record: Failure lineage and recovery attempts are retained per CAP-008 Data Implications. Every recovery carries its named permission, scoped support or incident authority, expected state version, reason, idempotency key and audit envelope.
- observability: Crawl, ingestion, parsing attempt and replay, failed-subset, readiness, Evaluation-transition and correlation telemetry.
- retention: Failure lineage and recovery attempts retained; the ingestion dead-letter staging bound is 24 hours. Window owned by 015 DATA_LIFECYCLE.md.
- test contracts: TYP-DATA: every admitted Crawl and parse member and every created IndexingJob has an exact terminal or scheduled outcome. TYP-DATA: each successful same-version job advances the Document exactly once. TYP-DATA: readiness derives from the complete parse and Evidence manifest WITHOUT waiting for the Retrieval projection - a lagging projection changes no readiness value. TYP-DATA: concurrent completion or replay cannot change an earlier Evaluation Input Snapshot. TYP-DATA: exhausted or nonretryable parse or indexing, blocked readiness, missing external input and denied recovery each create the enumerated state, reason, subset and events. TYP-SEC: no hidden retry exists - every retry is one of the bounded WF-005, `parsing-interim-v1` or `indexing-interim-v1` schedules and appears in the audit trace. TYP-DATA: no dropped manifest member, no stale Evaluation mutation, no unclassified persistence. TYP-SEC: recovery requires its named permission and, for a SecurityOperator, an active Support Session naming target and action; an unauthorized, stale or altered replay has no side effect. TYP-DATA: worker crash before retrieval - the lease expires and the work is reclaimed with no Document, no Evidence and no accounted bytes. TYP-DATA: crash during retrieval - the attempt is retried under the fixed schedule and accounted bytes are released. TYP-DATA: crash after retrieval but before durable handoff - no succeeded IngestionJob exists, so parsing never observes the artifact and the fetch is retried. TYP-DATA: crash after durable handoff - the succeeded job and its Evidence are committed, redelivery re-enters the same job identity and advances the Document no more than once. TYP-DATA: duplicate queue delivery produces no duplicate downstream effect. TYP-DATA: expired lease and stale heartbeat each release the claim without advancing state. TYP-DATA: partial frontier progress survives restart; successful subsets are preserved. TYP-DATA: `ingestion.recover` outside the 24-hour staging bound is denied. WITHHELD under OD-027 and asserted ABSENT: any test asserting a second IndexingJob per ParsingJob, a `has_one` narrowing, or a `unique (parsing_job_id)` constraint.
- rollout: The parsing handoff is consumed by S-08. The indexing multiplicity remains under `indexing-interim-v1` until OD-027 is ratified; completing the withheld limb requires that ratification and a controlled Volume I integration, not an implementation choice.

### MTX-009 - AC-CAP-009 (CAP: CAP-009)

- Slice: S-09
- Status: Complete
- contract owner: specification/volume-ii/APPLICATION_LAYER.md#cap-009-technical-inspection
- interface type: Capability-level obligation for `CHK-TI-001` execution, discharged by the PRULE-010 contract. CAP-009 defines no interface of its own.
- route: Not applicable - CAP-009 defines no interface independent of the WF-007 Check-execution path; see MTX-061.
- request schema: The frozen Evaluation Applicability Set entry for each active Source; exactly one valid `crawl_observation` Evidence per Source using schema `internal-link-observation-v1`, carrying Organization, Project, Source, Evaluation and Crawl IDs, canonical Source root, relevant-coverage status `full` or `partial`, and `targets` ordered by canonical target URL UTF-8 bytes; the exact Evidence and Validation Decisions; Catalog, Definition and policy versions; and the deterministic result key, per CAP-009 Inputs.
- response schema: One terminal Technical Integrity Check Result per applicable Source, per CAP-009 Outputs. Zero or more evidence-linked Issues follow only from a failed Result and are created by CAP-014 in S-12, not here.
- controller: Not applicable - no HTTP adapter.
- command: As MTX-061 - `MaterializeCheckResultKey` then `ExecuteCheckAttempt` for each `CHK-TI-001` expected entry. CAP-009 adds no command.
- command input: The frozen `CHK-TI-001` deterministic input tuple as MTX-061; cardinality is one Result per Source, subject type `source`, key the opaque Source ID unchanged, `source_id` nonnull.
- command output: One Check Result with pillar `technical_integrity` and `rule_or_model_version=CHK-TI-001-rule-v1`.
- actor: Tenant-scoped evaluation service identity; CAP-009 Actor is System automation.
- organization scope: The Evaluation's Organization; CAP-009 Preconditions require the immutable Evaluation input snapshot with exact full or partial coverage and failed-subset metadata.
- aggregate: CheckResult, with CheckApplicabilitySnapshot read-only as its input authority.
- aggregate boundary: As MTX-061 - a Check Result never spans into Issue creation.
- value objects: As MTX-061, plus CanonicalRoot, TargetTerminalStatus, TargetReason, ReferrerTuple, AbsentTargetKeys.
- domain service: Not applicable - CAP-009 names no domain service; the `CHK-TI-001` outcome rule is a pure function over the frozen payload.
- repository: `CheckResultRepository`, `CheckApplicabilitySnapshotRepository` read-only, `EvidenceRepository` read-only.
- persistence model: As MTX-061 - one immutable `check_results` row per retained preimage. Check outputs and evidence references are persisted per CAP-009 Data Implications; no new table is introduced.
- migration: Not applicable - no product schema object is added by this row.
- transaction boundary: As MTX-061 - identity materialized in one transaction before execution; the semantic output committed onto it in expected-key order.
- concurrency: As MTX-061 - one materialized identity per Source key; concurrent completion commits in expected-key order and cannot produce two Technical Integrity Results for one Source.
- idempotency: As MTX-061 - the retained preimage is authority; exact replay returns the stored Result and emits no second `CheckResultCreated`.
- background job: As MTX-061 - `CheckExecutionWorker` per materialized key.
- queue: As MTX-061 - the evaluation queue, owned by BACKGROUND_PROCESSING.md.
- retry policy: Common executor only, per CAP-009 Product Behavior: the five-second attempt ceiling and the single one-second retry for `check_dependency_unavailable` or `check_internal_timeout`. No provider call exists to retry.
- terminal failure: CAP-009 Failure Condition: missing, invalid or cross-tenant input yields the catalog-defined `error`; catalog, integrity or cardinality failure follows the WF-007 Evaluation failure path and never silently omits a Result. `error/internal_link_coverage_incomplete` with `error_reason_code=input_evidence_indeterminate` when relevant coverage is partial or any target is unobserved -- and that error PRECEDES failed and pass evaluation.
- reconciliation: Every expected `CHK-TI-001` entry -- one per active Source -- has exactly one terminal Result or the Evaluation fails. `absence_proof_mode=check_pass_resolves_all` against its exact frozen subject selector, so a valid passed replacement under full relevant coverage resolves only the predecessor for that same Definition and Source; the resolution itself is CAP-014's in S-12.
- domain events: `CheckResultCreated`; `CheckExecutionRetried` on the single permitted retry. CAP-009 adds no event.
- event payload: As MTX-061.
- event producer: `Workflows::Wf007`.
- event consumers: S-12 at Issue derivation; S-13 as `technical_integrity` pillar coverage.
- serializer: Not applicable - no transport exposure.
- authorization entry point: The evaluation-service command, before mutation.
- permission checks: Tenant-scoped evaluation service identity only; CAP-009 Security Implications state internal-only processing under tenant isolation and name no principal permission.
- tenant boundary: As MTX-061. CAP-009 Security Implications: internal-only processing under tenant isolation. A target outside the frozen Source Scope invalidates the payload rather than being evaluated.
- error contract: `error/internal_link_coverage_incomplete` with `error_reason_code=input_evidence_indeterminate` for partial relevant coverage or any unobserved target; the common handled reasons `input_evidence_missing`, `input_evidence_stale`, `input_evidence_invalid`, `normalized_input_invalid`, `output_schema_invalid`; retryable `check_dependency_unavailable` and `check_internal_timeout`. Payload invalidity -- duplicate target URLs or referrer tuples, a target outside frozen Source Scope, inconsistent status and reason, or noncanonical ordering -- makes the Evidence invalid and produces `input_evidence_invalid`. Catalog and applicability failures are MTX-061's and fail the Evaluation.
- audit record: As MTX-061, plus the normalized observation `source_id`, `canonical_root`, `total_target_count`, `reachable_count`, `absent_count`, `unobserved_count`, `absent_target_keys` and `absent_targets` with ordered referrers.
- observability: Evaluation and check-result telemetry, per CAP-009 Observability Requirements.
- retention: Check outputs and evidence references persisted per CAP-009 Data Implications; the window is owned by 015 DATA_LIFECYCLE.md.
- test contracts: TYP-DATA: outputs are exact -- `passed/internal_links_resolve` when coverage is full, every target is reachable and the absent set is empty; `failed/broken_internal_links` when coverage is full and one or more targets are absent; `error/internal_link_coverage_incomplete` with `error_reason_code=input_evidence_indeterminate` when relevant coverage is partial or any target is unobserved, and the error PRECEDES failed and pass evaluation. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-TI-001-01, -02): full coverage with an empty target set and full coverage with all targets reachable both pass. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-TI-001-03..-07): the impact boundaries of `impact-CHK-TI-001-v1` -- 1 absent -> `low`, 4 -> `low`, 5 -> `medium`, 19 -> `medium`, 20 -> `high` -- each asserted at the boundary and one either side. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-TI-001-08, -09): partial coverage and a single unobserved target each produce the error, not a pass. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-TI-001-10): a corrected same Source resolves the predecessor. TYP-DATA: the payload contract -- targets ordered by canonical target URL UTF-8 bytes; each target's terminal status and reason are consistent, with `reachable` requiring `document_valid`, `absent` requiring `content_absent` from terminal `404` or `410`, and every other reason requiring `unobserved`; each referrer carries Document ID, canonical referring URL and zero-based link position in normalized document order. TYP-DATA: duplicate target URLs, duplicate referrer tuples, a target outside frozen Source Scope, an inconsistent status/reason pair and noncanonical ordering each invalidate the payload -- five fixtures. TYP-DATA: normalized observation counts equal the corresponding arrays and target-key arrays use canonical target URL order. TYP-DATA: cardinality is one Result per active Source, subject type `source`, key the opaque Source ID unchanged, `source_id` nonnull, `document_id` null. TYP-DATA: `effort-interim-v1` maps 1-4 absent to `low`, 5-19 to `medium`, 20 or more to `high`, with `effort_basis` exactly `effort-interim-v1:CHK-TI-001:<outcome_code>:<matched-rule>`. TYP-DATA: passed, failed and not-applicable results use confidence `1.0000`, `confidence_status=valid`, band `high`; `rule_or_model_version` is exactly `CHK-TI-001-rule-v1` and no model is invoked. TYP-DATA: an attempted `not_applicable` from `CHK-TI-001` is `check_catalog_integrity_failure`. TYP-SEC: no provider call and no network request occurs during execution. TYP-SEC: invalid, missing, quarantined or cross-Organization Evidence produces no decision-grade Result -- the first three produce the handled error, the fourth fails the Evaluation.
- rollout: As MTX-061. `CHK-TI-001` consumes the S-08 `crawl_observation/internal-link-observation-v1` Evidence and the frozen Crawl terminal-outcome map from S-07; no Measurement Set is involved, so this Definition reaches a decision-grade pass or fail at the baseline.

### MTX-010 - AC-CAP-010 (CAP: CAP-010)

- Slice: S-09
- Status: Complete
- contract owner: specification/volume-ii/APPLICATION_LAYER.md#cap-010-content-and-external-presence-inspection
- interface type: Capability-level obligation for the five Definitions `CHK-CQ-001`, `CHK-SP-001`, `CHK-AIP-001`, `CHK-AS-001` and `CHK-LP-001`, discharged by the PRULE-010 contract. CAP-010 defines no interface of its own.
- route: Not applicable - CAP-010 defines no interface independent of the WF-007 Check-execution path; see MTX-061.
- request schema: `parsed_content/document-title-observation-v1` Evidence per successfully parsed Document for `CHK-CQ-001`; provider-neutral immutable `external_measurement/external-observation-v1` Evidence for `CHK-SP-001`, `CHK-AIP-001`, `CHK-AS-001` and applicable `CHK-LP-001`, plus the frozen `local-business-profile-v1` snapshot for `CHK-LP-001`; the frozen Project profile `local_presence_applicable` decision with its nonblank reason when false; the exact Evidence decisions; and Catalog, Definition and policy versions, per CAP-010 Inputs.
- response schema: Terminal Check Results for the five owned Definitions, per CAP-010 Outputs. Candidate or open Issues follow only from failed Results and are created by CAP-014 in S-12.
- controller: Not applicable - no HTTP adapter.
- command: As MTX-061 - `MaterializeCheckResultKey` then `ExecuteCheckAttempt` per expected entry for each of the five Definitions. CAP-010 adds no command.
- command input: Each Definition's frozen deterministic input tuple as MTX-061. `CHK-CQ-001` is one Result per Document with subject type `url` and key the canonical Document URL, Source and Document IDs nonnull. `CHK-SP-001`, `CHK-AIP-001`, `CHK-AS-001` and `CHK-LP-001` are one Result per Project each, subject type `project`, key the Project ID, Source and Document IDs null.
- command output: Five terminal Check Results in pillars `content_quality`, `search_presence`, `ai_presence`, `authority_signals` and `local_presence` respectively, plus one per parsed Document for `CHK-CQ-001`.
- actor: Tenant-scoped evaluation service identity; CAP-010 Actor is System automation.
- organization scope: The Evaluation's Organization. CAP-010 Preconditions require the input snapshot and applicability set sealed, with required parsed or `external-observation-v1` Evidence either frozen or explicitly absent or indeterminate. CAP-009 completion is expressly NOT a prerequisite and concurrent completion order is irrelevant.
- aggregate: CheckResult, with CheckApplicabilitySnapshot and the frozen `local-business-profile-v1` snapshot read-only as inputs.
- aggregate boundary: As MTX-061. Measurement Evidence intake is a WF-006 command owned by S-08 and commits before the snapshot seals; this row only consumes what the applicability entry selected.
- value objects: As MTX-061, plus TitleNodePosition, NonblankTitleCount, MeasurementKind, MeasurementSetVersion, CollectorAdapterId, CollectorAdapterVersion, CoverageStatus, FreshUntilUtc, PresenceRate, QualifiedRate, AttributableCount, RequiredListingKey, ListingStatus.
- domain service: Not applicable - CAP-010 names no domain service; each Definition's outcome rule is a pure function over its frozen payload.
- repository: `CheckResultRepository`, `CheckApplicabilitySnapshotRepository` read-only, `EvidenceRepository` read-only, `MeasurementSetRepository` read-only, `ProjectProfileRepository` read-only.
- persistence model: As MTX-061. Content-derived Issues and confidence metadata are persisted per CAP-010 Data Implications; the Issues themselves are CAP-014's records in S-12.
- migration: Not applicable - no product schema object is added by this row.
- transaction boundary: As MTX-061.
- concurrency: As MTX-061. CAP-010 Preconditions make CAP-009 completion irrelevant, so the two capabilities' Results may commit in any interleaving without changing either output; each is materialized on its own key.
- idempotency: As MTX-061 - the retained preimage is authority per Definition and subject; exact replay returns the stored Result.
- background job: As MTX-061.
- queue: As MTX-061.
- retry policy: Common executor only. CAP-010 Product Behavior: never call or select a provider during Check execution. The collector is outside Check execution for all four measurement-fed Definitions, so a stale or missing observation is a handled error rather than a retryable dependency.
- terminal failure: CAP-010 Failure Condition: missing, stale, indeterminate, malformed or invalid Evidence yields the Definition's exact `error`; catalog, cardinality or integrity failure fails the Evaluation without a partial current Issue set. Under the ratified OD-010 baseline, `CHK-SP-001`, `CHK-AIP-001` and `CHK-AS-001` deterministically select no Evidence and each persists a handled one-attempt `input_evidence_missing` error; `CHK-LP-001` does the same only when local presence is applicable and otherwise persists `not_applicable/local_presence_not_applicable`. That is the approved baseline outcome, not a failure.
- reconciliation: Every expected entry for the five Definitions has exactly one terminal Result. Each uses `absence_proof_mode=check_pass_resolves_all` against its exact frozen subject selector. An `error` never proves absence and never resolves an Issue; three of the four measurement-fed pillars are therefore `insufficient_data` at the baseline, which is exactly what makes the overall score unavailable.
- domain events: `CheckResultCreated`; `CheckExecutionRetried` on the single permitted retry. CAP-010 adds no event.
- event payload: As MTX-061.
- event producer: `Workflows::Wf007`.
- event consumers: S-12 at Issue derivation; S-13 as `content_quality`, `search_presence`, `ai_presence`, `authority_signals` and `local_presence` pillar coverage.
- serializer: Not applicable - no transport exposure.
- authorization entry point: The evaluation-service command, before mutation.
- permission checks: Tenant-scoped evaluation service identity only; CAP-010 Security Implications state tenant-scoped evidence access and name no principal permission. `external_measurement.submit` and `measurement_set.activate` govern WF-006 intake and are owned by S-08; neither is reachable from Check execution.
- tenant boundary: As MTX-061. A cross-Organization, cross-Project or cross-Evaluation `external-observation-v1` payload is tenant-integrity failure and fails the Evaluation through the applicability boundary rather than creating a handled Result -- this is the one place the measurement path escalates instead of degrading. Provider text, prompts, unrestricted responses, credentials and opaque error strings are never retained in the payload.
- error contract: Measurement intake mapping, exact: a missing payload produces `input_evidence_missing`; a stale payload produces `input_evidence_stale`; partial coverage, any indeterminate item, or a key-set mismatch produces `input_evidence_indeterminate`; invalid same-tenant digest, schema or provenance produces `input_evidence_invalid`. All four are handled Check errors with no Issue that make the applicable pillar insufficient. Freshness is exact: an Evaluation Input Snapshot may consume an observation only when `observed_at_utc <= snapshot_sealed_at_utc < fresh_until_utc`, and equality at `fresh_until_utc` is STALE. `CHK-CQ-001` payload invalidity -- duplicate positions, unresolved locators, unsupported media type, or a value inconsistent with the normalized source bytes -- produces `input_evidence_invalid`. Catalog and applicability failures are MTX-061's.
- audit record: As MTX-061, plus each Definition's normalized observation: `CHK-CQ-001` canonical URL, `title_node_count`, `nonblank_title_count` and ordered node positions, locators and normalized values; `CHK-SP-001` measurement, adapter and set versions, `expected_count`, `present_count`, `absent_count`, `presence_rate` and `absent_query_keys`; `CHK-AIP-001` the same versions plus `expected_count`, `qualified_count`, `unqualified_count`, `qualified_rate` and `unqualified_intent_keys` with presence and citation statuses; `CHK-AS-001` `backlink_count`, `brand_mention_count`, `attributable_count`, `nonattributable_count`, `total_count` and sorted attributable observation keys; `CHK-LP-001` `required_count`, `present_count`, `qualified_count` and `nonqualified_listing_keys`, or the Project profile version and reason when not applicable.
- observability: `EvaluationCompleted` plus content-check metrics, per CAP-010 Observability Requirements. The three baseline `input_evidence_missing` errors and the resulting insufficient pillars are observable as exact reason codes rather than as a silent absence.
- retention: Content-derived Issues and confidence metadata persisted per CAP-010 Data Implications; the window is owned by 015 DATA_LIFECYCLE.md.
- test contracts: TYP-DATA (`CHK-CQ-001`): `failed/meta_title_missing` at zero nonblank normalized titles, `passed/meta_title_present` at one, `failed/meta_title_multiple` at two or more; impact `medium` for missing and `low` for multiple; effort `low`; template `REC-CHK-CQ-001-v1`. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-CQ-001-01..-06): zero nodes -> missing/medium; blank-only -> missing/medium; one nonblank with surrounding and repeated Unicode whitespace -> normalized pass; two nonblank -> multiple/low; invalid locator -> error; corrected same URL -> resolved. TYP-DATA: title normalization decodes character references, converts to Unicode NFC, trims leading and trailing Unicode White_Space, and replaces every internal run of one or more White_Space characters with one ASCII space; blank normalized values REMAIN in the node list. TYP-DATA (`CHK-SP-001`): `presence_rate=present_count/expected_count` to four decimals round half up; `passed/search_presence_complete` requires exactly `1.0000`, otherwise `failed/search_presence_gap`; impact `high` at rate `0.0000` and `medium` for `0.0000 < rate < 1.0000`; effort `medium`. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-SP-001-01..-09): empty expected set -> error; key mismatch -> error; partial -> error; indeterminate -> error; stale with equality at expiry -> error; all present -> pass; zero present -> high; partial presence -> medium; array permutation -> identical normalization. TYP-DATA (`CHK-AIP-001`): a qualified item is present, cited and has at least one entity key; `qualified_rate=qualified_count/expected_count` to four decimals round half up; `passed/ai_answer_presence_complete` requires `1.0000`; impact `high` at `0.0000`, `medium` in between; effort `high`. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-AIP-001-01..-06), including present-but-not-cited -> unqualified. TYP-DATA (`CHK-AS-001`): `passed/authority_reference_present` when attributable count is at least one, otherwise `failed/authority_reference_absent` with impact `medium`; effort `high`. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-AS-001-01..-06): an EMPTY COMPLETE list is a valid zero-signal observation that fails at `medium`, not an error; one attributable backlink passes; one attributable mention passes; only nonattributable fails; indeterminate errors; a duplicate tuple is invalid. TYP-DATA (`CHK-LP-001`): `not_applicable/local_presence_not_applicable` ONLY from the valid frozen false applicability decision with its nonblank reason; otherwise `passed/local_profiles_consistent` when every required listing is qualified, `failed/local_profiles_absent` at zero present listings, else `failed/local_profile_inconsistent`; impact `high` for all absent and `medium` for partial or any inconsistency; effort `medium`; no Artifact for not-applicable. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-LP-001-01..-06): valid false applicability -> not-applicable WITHOUT Measurement Evidence; false without reason -> applicability failure; true with missing, stale, partial or indeterminate Measurement Evidence -> the exact error; zero present -> high; partial, mismatch or missing field -> medium; all required present with all fields matching -> pass. TYP-DATA: `not_applicable` is valid ONLY for `CHK-LP-001`; an attempted not-applicable from `CHK-CQ-001`, `CHK-SP-001`, `CHK-AIP-001` or `CHK-AS-001` is `check_catalog_integrity_failure` -- four fixtures. TYP-DATA: the `external-observation-v1` envelope is exact -- `measurement_policy_version=external-measurement-interim-v1`, `locale=en-AU`, `time_zone=UTC`, `fresh_until_utc` exactly 24 elapsed hours after `observed_at_utc`, the envelope `observed_at_utc` equal to the payload value, and `captured_at_utc` at or after observation and before freshness expiry. TYP-DATA: freshness boundary -- consumable when `observed_at_utc <= snapshot_sealed_at_utc < fresh_until_utc`; equality at `fresh_until_utc` is STALE; asserted immediately before, exactly at, and immediately after. TYP-DATA: each kind-specific body is exact -- `search_index_presence` nonempty `expected_query_keys` sorted by UTF-8 bytes with exactly one item per key in that order, `present` requiring at least one URL and `absent` requiring none; `ai_answer_presence` with `absent` requiring `not_cited` and no entity key; `authority_reference_set` sorted by canonical referrer, reference type, canonical target then observation key with exact duplicate tuples invalid; `local_profile_consistency` bound to the exact frozen `local-business-profile-v1` snapshot ID and content hash, with an absent listing requiring all four comparisons `missing` and a qualified listing requiring all four `match`. TYP-DATA (OD-010 ratified baseline): `external-measurement-v1` bundles no active Measurement Set, so `CHK-SP-001`, `CHK-AIP-001` and `CHK-AS-001` each deterministically select no Evidence and persist a handled one-attempt `input_evidence_missing` error with no Issue and `subject_set_complete=false`; `CHK-LP-001` does the same only when local presence is applicable and otherwise returns its catalog-defined not-applicable result without Evidence. TYP-DATA: those three insufficient pillars make the overall numeric score unavailable, and this is asserted as the APPROVED baseline outcome rather than a defect or a withheld limb. TYP-SEC: no implementation invents a query, intent, listing directory, prompt, provider, threshold or adapter to avoid that outcome. TYP-SEC: no Check selects or calls a network provider; the collector is outside Check execution for all four measurement-fed Definitions. TYP-SEC: a cross-Organization, cross-Project or cross-Evaluation payload fails the Evaluation through the applicability boundary rather than creating a handled Result. TYP-DATA: `CHK-CQ-001` cardinality is one Result per successfully parsed Document with subject type `url`; the four Project Definitions are one Result each with subject type `project`, key the Project ID, Source and Document IDs null. TYP-AI: `CHK-AIP-001` measures AI answer presence from a frozen provider-neutral observation and invokes no model; `rule_or_model_version` is exactly `CHK-AIP-001-rule-v1`.
- rollout: As MTX-061. Activating a Measurement Set later is exact signed configuration under `external-measurement-v1` and changes no code path in this row: the same four Definitions then select frozen Evidence instead of returning `input_evidence_missing`, and Check execution still makes no provider call.

### MTX-011 - AC-CAP-011 (CAP: CAP-011)

- Slice: S-09
- Status: Complete
- contract owner: specification/volume-ii/APPLICATION_LAYER.md#cap-011-structured-data-inspection
- interface type: Capability-level obligation for `CHK-TR-001` execution, discharged by the PRULE-010 contract. CAP-011 defines no interface of its own.
- route: Not applicable - CAP-011 defines no interface independent of the WF-007 Check-execution path; see MTX-061.
- request schema: The frozen Organization `organization-profile-v1`, the Source canonical root, exactly one valid `parsed_content` Evidence per active Source root using schema `organization-identity-observation-v1` with its Organization nodes, the Evidence decisions, Definition and policy versions, and the applicability key, per CAP-011 Inputs.
- response schema: One terminal Trust Signals Check Result per applicable Source, per CAP-011 Outputs. An evidence-linked Issue follows only from a failed Result and is created by CAP-014 in S-12.
- controller: Not applicable - no HTTP adapter.
- command: As MTX-061 - `MaterializeCheckResultKey` then `ExecuteCheckAttempt` per `CHK-TR-001` expected entry. CAP-011 adds no command.
- command input: The frozen `CHK-TR-001` deterministic input tuple as MTX-061; cardinality is one Result per Source, subject type `source`, key the Source ID.
- command output: One Check Result with pillar `trust_signals` and `rule_or_model_version=CHK-TR-001-rule-v1`.
- actor: Tenant-scoped evaluation service identity; CAP-011 Actor is System automation.
- organization scope: The Evaluation's Organization; CAP-011 Preconditions require parsed structured-data artifacts to be available.
- aggregate: CheckResult, with CheckApplicabilitySnapshot and the frozen `public-identity-interim-v1` snapshot read-only as inputs.
- aggregate boundary: As MTX-061.
- value objects: As MTX-061, plus PublicIdentityProfileId, PublicIdentityProfileVersion, ExpectedPublicName, ExpectedPublicUrl, OrganizationNodePosition, SchemaValid, NodeCount, ValidNodeCount, MatchingNodeCount.
- domain service: Not applicable - CAP-011 names no domain service; the identity-presence and consistency rule is a pure function over the frozen payload.
- repository: `CheckResultRepository`, `CheckApplicabilitySnapshotRepository` read-only, `EvidenceRepository` read-only, `PublicIdentityProfileRepository` read-only.
- persistence model: As MTX-061. Schema validation outcomes and evidence pointers are persisted per CAP-011 Data Implications.
- migration: Not applicable - no product schema object is added by this row.
- transaction boundary: As MTX-061.
- concurrency: As MTX-061 - one materialized identity per Source key; concurrent completion cannot produce two Trust Signals Results for one Source.
- idempotency: As MTX-061 - exact input hash, collision and replay behavior per CAP-011 Product Behavior, with the retained preimage as authority.
- background job: As MTX-061.
- queue: As MTX-061.
- retry policy: Common executor only, per CAP-011 Product Behavior: the five-second ceiling and the single one-second retry. No provider call exists.
- terminal failure: CAP-011 Failure Condition: invalid schema, input integrity, tenant or Evidence yields the catalog-defined `error` and no decision-grade Issue; catalog or cardinality failure fails the Evaluation. A Source whose root input is missing still has an expected entry, so it produces an explicit handled error rather than disappearing.
- reconciliation: Every expected `CHK-TR-001` entry -- one per active Source, including a root-missing Source -- has exactly one terminal Result. `absence_proof_mode=check_pass_resolves_all` against its frozen Source selector.
- domain events: `CheckResultCreated`; `CheckExecutionRetried` on the single permitted retry. CAP-011 adds no event.
- event payload: As MTX-061.
- event producer: `Workflows::Wf007`.
- event consumers: S-12 at Issue derivation; S-13 as `trust_signals` pillar coverage.
- serializer: Not applicable - no transport exposure.
- authorization entry point: The evaluation-service command, before mutation.
- permission checks: Tenant-scoped evaluation service identity only; CAP-011 Security Implications are `None beyond standard tenant isolation` and name no principal permission.
- tenant boundary: As MTX-061. The expected public name is the Organization's normalized display name and the expected public URL is the Source canonical HTTPS root, both resolved inside the Evaluation's Organization; a tenant mismatch prevents decision-grade output.
- error contract: Handled: missing expected identity or profile values, an ambiguous profile version, an invalid locator, or inconsistent normalization invalidates the payload and produces `input_evidence_invalid`; a missing root input produces `input_evidence_missing`. ZERO OBSERVED NODES IS A VALID OBSERVATION, not an error: it is `failed/organization_schema_missing`. Catalog and applicability failures are MTX-061's.
- audit record: As MTX-061, plus the normalized observation `source_id`, `canonical_root`, `public_identity_profile_id`, `public_identity_profile_version`, `node_count`, `valid_node_count`, `matching_node_count`, and ordered node positions and locators with schema validity and field-match booleans. MTX-064 owns this persistence obligation in full.
- observability: Parsing and validation success or failure metrics, per CAP-011 Observability Requirements.
- retention: Schema validation outcomes and evidence pointers persisted per CAP-011 Data Implications; the window is owned by 015 DATA_LIFECYCLE.md.
- test contracts: TYP-DATA: outputs in FIRST-MATCH order -- `failed/organization_schema_missing` for zero nodes; `passed/organization_identity_consistent` when at least one node is schema-valid and both normalized values exactly match; `failed/organization_schema_invalid` when no node is schema-valid; otherwise `failed/organization_identity_mismatch`. The order is asserted with a fixture where two branches would both match. TYP-DATA (FX-CHK-TR-001-01..-06): zero nodes -> missing/medium; no schema-valid node -> invalid/medium; valid mismatch -> mismatch/high; one exact matching valid node AMONG OTHERS -> pass; missing profile -> error; corrected same Source -> resolved. TYP-DATA: `impact-CHK-TR-001-v1` maps missing or invalid to `medium` and identity mismatch to `high`, asserted at each boundary. TYP-DATA: public-name normalization uses Unicode NFC, the same whitespace rule as title normalization, and Unicode 15.1 default full case folding for comparison; URL comparison uses the canonical URL rules of the score and evidence model. TYP-DATA: a valid matching node has `schema_valid=true` AND both normalized values equal to the expected values -- both clauses asserted independently. TYP-DATA: zero observed nodes is a VALID observation producing a failed Result, not an invalid payload. TYP-DATA: missing expected identity or profile values, an ambiguous profile version, an invalid locator and inconsistent normalization each invalidate the payload -- four fixtures. TYP-DATA: cardinality is one Result per active Source including a Source whose root input is missing, which yields an explicit handled error rather than an omitted entry. TYP-DATA: effort is `medium` for every failure; failed outcomes render `REC-CHK-TR-001-v1`. TYP-DATA: `rule_or_model_version` is exactly `CHK-TR-001-rule-v1` and no model is invoked. TYP-DATA: an attempted `not_applicable` from `CHK-TR-001` is `check_catalog_integrity_failure`. TYP-SEC: unsupported schema, invalid Evidence or tenant mismatch prevents decision-grade output; a cross-Organization reference fails the Evaluation. TYP-SEC: no provider call and no network request occurs during execution; JSON-LD remote contexts were never fetched at parse time either, which is S-08's assertion carried forward.
- rollout: As MTX-061. `CHK-TR-001` consumes the S-08 `parsed_content/organization-identity-observation-v1` Evidence derived from a frozen `public-identity-interim-v1` snapshot; no Measurement Set is involved, so this Definition reaches a decision-grade pass or fail at the baseline.

### MTX-012 - AC-CAP-012 (CAP: CAP-012)

- Slice: S-10
- Status: Complete
- contract owner: specification/volume-ii/APPLICATION_LAYER.md#cap-012-ai-discoverability-analysis
- interface type: AI orchestration commands plus the tenant-scoped citation-validation service. CAP-012's Actor is the AI orchestration service, with an OrganizationAdmin or MarketingOperator for publication control. At the ratified baseline the generation limb is reachable only in `deterministic_template` mode; an `ai_assisted` request fails closed at the provider gate before entitlement reservation or provider call.
- route: Not applicable - Volume I defines the commands, their permissions and their envelopes but no HTTP path, method or transport. Transport exposure is owned by API_CONTRACTS.md at the Volume II baseline. No inbound provider callback exists: the AIResponse lifecycle is driven by the orchestrating worker that made the request, so an inbound callback would be an unauthenticated surface with no authority behind it.
- request schema: AIResponse request: Organization and Project IDs, target `recommendation_id` and `artifact_version`, `origin_issue_id` and its state version, ordered input Evidence ID/content-digest/Validation-Decision tuples, prompt template ID/version, model ID/version, response-policy and citation-policy versions, locale, `request_fingerprint_sha256` with its retained preimage, idempotency key, and correlation ID.
- response schema: The AIResponse with its status, nullable rejection reason, the four due instants, immutable generated-payload reference and content digest, ordered claim manifest, state version and correlation ID; plus its Citations with their statuses and first-match reason codes. At the baseline an `ai_assisted` request returns `F1-AI-422 / ai_provider_unapproved` and no AIResponse record.
- controller: Not applicable - no HTTP adapter is defined by Volume I; see route.
- command: `Workflows::Wf009::RequestAiResponse`, `Workflows::Wf009::PersistGeneratedPayload`, `Workflows::Wf009::ProposeCitations`, `Workflows::Wf009::ValidateAiResponse` (the single validation transaction), `Workflows::Wf009::RejectAiResponse`, `Workflows::Wf009::ExpireAiResponse`, `Workflows::Wf009::SupersedeCitations`. `RequestAiResponse` is where the provider gate fires. Recommendation Artifact generation, versioning and publication are WF-009 commands owned by S-14.
- command input: As request_schema. The prompt envelope, when an approved signed artifact is later active, places immutable Evidence ONLY in a delimited untrusted-data field, never in system or developer instructions; strips executable markup and provider tool directives; sends no credentials, verification material, restricted Evidence or field above the artifact's classification allowlist; fixes the response to the exact Recommendation schema and claim manifest; and rejects unknown or extra fields.
- command output: Requested, generated, validated, rejected or expired AIResponse attempts and proposed, verified, invalid or superseded Citations with immutable claim and Evidence lineage, per CAP-012 Outputs.
- actor: AI orchestration service identity for the request and generation limbs; the tenant-scoped citation-validation service for every Citation decision; an OrganizationAdmin or MarketingOperator holding `recommendation.publish` for publication control, which is S-14's command. A TechnicalImplementer is a consumer and cannot publish.
- organization scope: The origin Issue's Organization and Project. Every AIResponse, Citation and input Evidence record resolves inside it; the AIResponse carries Organization and Project IDs explicitly and the Citation carries them too.
- aggregate: AIResponse as one root, Citation as a separate root belonging to exactly one AIResponse. RecommendationArtifact is the binding target and is a separate root owned by S-14; Issue and CheckResult are read-only antecedents.
- aggregate boundary: The validation transaction is the one bounded multi-root commit: one transaction persists a terminal decision for EVERY proposed Citation and changes the AIResponse to `validated` on full coverage or `rejected` on failure. Binding validated draft content to the target Artifact version happens only on full pass; on failure the rejected response and Citation decisions are retained for audit and no AI-assisted Artifact publishes. The AIResponse never writes to Evaluation: under OD-007 neither AIResponse nor Citation stores a direct Evaluation link.
- value objects: AiResponseId, RequestFingerprintSha256, RequestFingerprintPreimage, PromptTemplateId, PromptTemplateVersion, ModelId, ModelVersion, ResponsePolicyVersion, CitationPolicyVersion, ClaimKey, ClaimSha256, ClaimManifest, GeneratedPayloadDigest, GenerationDueAtUtc, ValidationDueAtUtc, PublicationExpiresAtUtc, CitationId, EvidenceLocator, CitationFingerprintSha256, CitationFingerprintPreimage, AiResponseStatus, CitationStatus.
- domain service: The tenant-scoped citation-validation service is named by Volume I and is the sole decider of every `proposed -> verified` and `proposed -> invalid` transition. It is not a convenience object: its identity is persisted on each Citation as the deciding service, so a Citation decision is attributable.
- repository: `AiResponseRepository`, `CitationRepository`, `IssueRepository` read-only, `CheckResultRepository` read-only, `EvidenceRepository` read-only, `EvidenceValidationDecisionRepository` read-only, `RecommendationArtifactRepository` read-only at the binding boundary, `AiPolicyArtifactRepository` read-only for the signed `ai-response-interim-v1` and `ai-safety-interim-v1` artifacts.
- persistence model: `ai_responses` unique on `(organization_id, request_fingerprint_version, request_fingerprint_preimage)` -- the retained preimage, not `request_fingerprint_sha256`, is uniqueness authority, so the unique index MUST NOT be built on the hash alone. `citations` unique on the retained Citation preimage within one AIResponse, with `ai_response_id` NOT NULL, `evidence_id` NOT NULL and NO `evaluation_id` column at all: under OD-007 the absence is structural, not a nullable convenience. The generated payload is an immutable reference with a content digest; the claim manifest is ordered and immutable once generated. A same-hash/different-preimage record for either type never merges and holds its own row plus restricted collision telemetry.
- migration: Not applicable at this row - the canonical schema owns `ai_responses` and `citations` per schemas/POSTGRESQL_SCHEMA.md. No product schema object is added. An `evaluation_id` column on either table MUST NOT be migrated: OD-007 ratified the one-directional model and a direct Evaluation write link is a prohibited transformation.
- transaction boundary: Request: one transaction creating or replaying the fingerprinted AIResponse at `requested`; at the baseline the provider gate rejects before this transaction opens, so no record is created. Generation: one transaction atomically persisting the generated payload, its digest, the ordered claim manifest and the proposed Citations. Validation: ONE transaction persisting a terminal decision for every proposed Citation, changing the AIResponse to validated or rejected, and binding draft content to the target Artifact version only on full pass. The `ai.generate` durable entitlement commit MUST commit strictly before `validation_due_at_utc`. No provider call sits inside a database transaction.
- concurrency: The request fingerprint serializes the attempt: exact preimage replay returns the original attempt and never calls the provider or validates twice. Concurrent exact requests resolve to one attempt. The validation transaction is atomic over the complete Citation set, so a partially decided set is not observable. Publication atomically binds the validated AIResponse to the target Artifact version and cancels expiry; a bound response remains `validated` as immutable lineage. Entitlement reservation for `ai.generate` uses a 15-minute prestart lifetime and a 2-minute maximum execution with a 2-minute heartbeat, so a lost orchestrator releases rather than strands the unit.
- idempotency: The AIResponse request-fingerprint preimage is canonical JSON containing exactly Organization, Project, target Recommendation and version, origin Issue and state version, ordered Evidence tuples, prompt template and version, model and version, response-policy and citation-policy versions, and locale. Exact preimage replay returns the original attempt with no provider call and no second validation. Reuse of its idempotency key with a DIFFERENT preimage is `idempotency_conflict`. A same hash with a different retained preimage never merges and emits restricted `AIResponseFingerprintCollision` telemetry. The Citation fingerprint preimage is canonical JSON containing exactly Organization, Project, AIResponse ID, Evidence ID with content digest and Validation Decision ID and status, claim key and digest, normalized evidence locator, and citation-policy version; within one AIResponse the full retained preimage is uniqueness authority, exact replay returns one Citation and one event outcome, and a same-hash/different-preimage record emits restricted `CitationFingerprintCollision` telemetry without merging.
- background job: `AiResponseGenerationWorker` per requested attempt, holding the `ai.generate` reservation lease; `AiResponseGenerationTimeoutJob` at `generation_due_at_utc`; `AiResponseValidationTimeoutJob` at `validation_due_at_utc`; `AiResponseExpiryJob` at `publication_expires_at_utc`. At the baseline none of these runs, because `RequestAiResponse` fails closed at the gate before an attempt exists.
- queue: The AI generation queue, per the Volume I Background Processing action registry. Queue identity and worker topology are owned by BACKGROUND_PROCESSING.md; this row does not rename them.
- retry policy: Provider timeout and retry remain exactly those in the AIResponse lifecycle; NO hidden provider retry is allowed. A provider or validation retry creates a NEW AIResponse attempt linked by causation ID and never reopens a terminal response. `rejected` and `expired` are terminal. `ai_provider_unapproved` is nonretryable in the sense that recovery is selecting deterministic generation or activating an approved signed artifact, not re-submitting the same request.
- terminal failure: `requested -> rejected` for `generation_timeout`, `provider_failure` or `policy_denied`. `generated -> rejected` for `schema_invalid`, `citation_missing`, `citation_invalid`, `stale_input`, `prohibited_advisory_domain`, `policy_denied` or `validation_timeout`. Unpublished `validated -> expired`. These transitions are exhaustive. Rejected and expired are terminal; a corrected linkage is a new Citation and a corrected attempt is a new AIResponse.
- reconciliation: One validation transaction ALWAYS persists a terminal decision for every proposed Citation, so no Citation is left proposed after validation. AIResponse rejection or validation timeout changes any remaining proposed Citation to `invalid` with `response_rejected` or `validation_timeout` in the same transaction. `verified -> superseded` with reason `artifact_version_superseded` occurs only when a later published Artifact version replaces the target version; invalid and superseded are terminal.
- domain events: `AIResponseRequested`, `AIResponseGenerated`, `AIResponseValidated`, `AIResponseRejected`, `AIResponseExpired`, `CitationProposed`, `CitationVerified`, `CitationInvalidated`, `CitationSuperseded`, restricted `AIResponseFingerprintCollision`, restricted `CitationFingerprintCollision`. `RecommendationArtifactGenerated`, `RecommendationPublished`, `RecommendationSuppressed` and `RecommendationRetired` are WF-009 Artifact events owned by S-14 and are not emitted here.
- event payload: The canonical envelope with the real `organization_id`, plus the AIResponse or Citation identity, target Recommendation and version, origin Issue and state version, prompt, model and policy versions, fingerprint outcome, claim key and digest for a Citation, the decision reason, the relevant due instant, and the correlation ID. Provider text, prompts, unrestricted responses, credentials and opaque error strings MUST NOT appear in any event payload or customer output; collision telemetry is restricted.
- event producer: `Workflows::Wf009` is the sole producer of the eleven events above. Provider latency, queue depth and lease state are technical telemetry and MUST NOT be emitted as domain events.
- event consumers: S-14 consumes the validated bound AIResponse as a publication predicate for an `ai_assisted` Artifact version, by reading it rather than by reacting to `AIResponseValidated`. No other consumer is named by Volume I.
- serializer: Not applicable - no transport exposure is defined by Volume I; see route.
- authorization entry point: The orchestration command, before mutation, and again at the `ai.generate` entitlement checkpoint immediately before the provider call. At the baseline the provider gate is evaluated BEFORE entitlement reservation, so a rejected `ai_assisted` request consumes no unit.
- permission checks: AI orchestration service identity for the request and generation limbs; the tenant-scoped citation-validation service identity for Citation decisions. `ai.generate` is the high-cost entitlement operation, counter group `ai.generate`, unit `validated_ai_response`, 1 unit, soft 20, hard 25, 15-minute prestart lifetime, 2-minute maximum execution, durable commit point `validated AIResponse is persisted for downstream use`. `recommendation.publish`, held by an OrganizationAdmin or MarketingOperator, governs publication and is S-14's check. Volume I defines no principal permission that reaches Citation validation, and none may be invented.
- tenant boundary: Organization and Project must match across the AIResponse, its target Artifact, its origin Issue, every input Evidence record and every Citation. `tenant_mismatch` is the FIRST reason in the Citation validation first-match order, so a cross-tenant Citation is rejected before any other check can mask it. The prompt envelope sends no credentials, verification material, restricted Evidence or field above the artifact's classification allowlist, and immutable Evidence is confined to a delimited untrusted-data field.
- error contract: Provider gate: `F1-AI-422 / ai_provider_unapproved`, before entitlement reservation or provider call, creating no AIResponse or Citation. AIResponse rejection reasons, exhaustive: `generation_timeout`, `provider_failure`, `policy_denied`, `schema_invalid`, `citation_missing`, `citation_invalid`, `stale_input`, `prohibited_advisory_domain`, `validation_timeout`. Output scanner first-match order, applied BEFORE Citation validation: `prompt_instruction_leakage`, `secret_or_personal_data`, `prohibited_advisory_domain`, `unsafe_external_action`, `output_schema_invalid`; any match changes the AIResponse to rejected with `policy_denied` EXCEPT the already canonical prohibited-advisory and schema reasons, publishes nothing, and permits only a new linked attempt after input or policy correction. Citation validation first-match order: `tenant_mismatch`, `claim_missing`, `claim_digest_mismatch`, `evidence_not_valid`, `evidence_digest_mismatch`, `locator_unresolved`, `evidence_not_in_origin_lineage`. Idempotency: `idempotency_conflict` on key reuse with a different preimage.
- audit record: Every AIResponse and Citation transition, collision, replay, timeout, coverage result and binding event, per CAP-012 Observability Requirements. Persisted: prompt, model and policy versions; response and Citation fingerprint preimages; the ordered claim manifest with claim keys and digests; the one-directional lineage; every decision with its deciding service identity and reason; and all timestamps, per CAP-012 Data Implications.
- observability: Every AIResponse and Citation transition, collision, replay, timeout, coverage result and binding event. The four due instants are observable so an expiry is attributable to a clock boundary rather than to a lost attempt. At the baseline, every `ai_provider_unapproved` rejection is observable with its exact reason, so the gate firing is a visible fact rather than a silent absence.
- retention: AIResponse and Citation records, their fingerprint preimages, claim manifests, decisions and timestamps are immutable lineage retained as `product_history`. The generated-payload reference is immutable. The retention window is owned by 015 DATA_LIFECYCLE.md; this row states none.
- test contracts: TYP-AI: at the baseline an `ai_assisted` request fails as `F1-AI-422 / ai_provider_unapproved` BEFORE entitlement reservation and before any provider call, and creates NO AIResponse and NO Citation -- asserted by observing zero `ai.generate` reservation, zero outbound provider sockets and zero rows. TYP-SEC: recovery is only by selecting deterministic generation or activating an approved signed artifact; no feature flag, tenant setting, configuration value or implementation choice weakens the gate -- asserted once per enablement vector. TYP-SEC: an implementation does not choose a provider or send Evidence anywhere while the gate holds. TYP-AI: with an active signed `ai-response-interim-v1` and `ai-safety-interim-v1` naming an approved provider adapter and model, data-handling approval, allowed Evidence Classifications, prompt-template hash, output schema, prohibited-content scanner and contract-review reference, the complete lifecycle below executes -- these fixtures are mandatory now, because the contract is specified now and the gate is what defers its reachability. TYP-DATA: every AIResponse required field is present and exact, including the four due instants. TYP-OBS: `generation_due_at_utc` is exactly 90 seconds after request and at exactly that instant TIMEOUT WINS over provider completion -- asserted immediately before, exactly at, and immediately after. TYP-OBS: `validation_due_at_utc` is exactly two minutes after request; validation AND the `ai.generate` durable entitlement commit must commit STRICTLY BEFORE it; at exactly that maximum-execution instant lease expiry and `validation_timeout` win over validation. TYP-OBS: `publication_expires_at_utc` is exactly 24 hours after validation and at exactly that instant expiry wins over publication. Every unlisted transition is rejected. TYP-DATA: rejected and expired are terminal; a provider or validation retry creates a NEW attempt linked by causation ID and never reopens a terminal response. TYP-DATA: the request-fingerprint preimage contains exactly Organization, Project, target Recommendation and version, origin Issue and state version, ordered Evidence tuples, prompt template and version, model and version, response-policy and citation-policy versions, and locale -- no more, no less. TYP-DATA: exact preimage replay returns the original attempt and never calls the provider or validates twice. TYP-DATA: idempotency-key reuse with a different preimage is `idempotency_conflict`. TYP-DATA: a same hash with a different retained preimage never merges and emits restricted `AIResponseFingerprintCollision`. TYP-DATA: the claim manifest has exactly one entry for EACH customer-visible value -- `problem_statement`, `rationale`, `expected_impact_band`, `confidence_value`, `confidence_band`, `effort_band`, `effort_basis`, `platform_applicability`, each `implementation_steps[i]` and each `verification_steps[i]` -- with a unique `claim_key` equal to that logical path and `claim_sha256` over its canonical UTF-8 value. TYP-AI: NO customer-visible generated value exists outside the manifest -- asserted by diffing the rendered output against the manifest key set. TYP-AI: `effort_band` is asserted present in the claim manifest of every AI-assisted version (AC-SM-001). TYP-DATA: Citation coverage passes ONLY when every claim has at least one verified Citation, every Citation targets a manifest claim, and no Citation for the response remains proposed or invalid -- three clauses, each asserted with an independent failing fixture. TYP-DATA (OD-007 ratified): a Citation belongs to exactly one AIResponse and points to exactly one Evidence record; a Citation with zero or multiple AIResponse or Evidence parents is not creatable. TYP-SEC (OD-007 ratified): neither AIResponse nor Citation stores a direct Evaluation link -- the column is asserted ABSENT from the schema, not merely null; evaluation traversal is `AIResponse -> Recommendation Artifact -> origin Issue -> Check Result/Evidence`. TYP-DATA: a read-model projection MAY denormalize that traversal but MUST NOT become write authority -- asserted by proving the projection has no write path. TYP-DATA: the Citation fingerprint preimage contains exactly Organization, Project, AIResponse ID, Evidence ID with content digest and Validation Decision ID and status, claim key and digest, normalized evidence locator, and citation-policy version. TYP-DATA: within one AIResponse the full retained preimage is uniqueness authority; exact replay returns one Citation and one event outcome; a same-hash/different-preimage record never merges and emits restricted `CitationFingerprintCollision`. TYP-DATA: `proposed -> verified` requires ALL of Organization and Project match, the AIResponse claim existing with the same digest, the Evidence effectively valid with the frozen digest and Decision, the locator resolving inside the immutable Evidence payload, and the Evidence being directly in the origin Issue or its Check Result lineage -- five clauses, each with an independent failing fixture. TYP-DATA: the failure first-match order is exactly `tenant_mismatch`, `claim_missing`, `claim_digest_mismatch`, `evidence_not_valid`, `evidence_digest_mismatch`, `locator_unresolved`, `evidence_not_in_origin_lineage` -- asserted with a fixture where two reasons would both apply. TYP-DATA: `verified -> superseded` occurs only when a later published Artifact version replaces the target version, with reason `artifact_version_superseded`; invalid and superseded are terminal and a corrected linkage is a new Citation. TYP-DATA: ONE validation transaction always persists a terminal decision for EVERY proposed Citation and changes the AIResponse to validated on full coverage or rejected on failure; a partially decided set is asserted unobservable. TYP-DATA: the transaction binds validated draft content to the target Artifact version only on FULL pass; on failure it retains the rejected response and Citation decisions for audit and publishes no AI-assisted Artifact. TYP-DATA: publication atomically binds the validated AIResponse to the target version and cancels expiry; a bound response remains `validated` as immutable lineage. TYP-SEC: the prompt envelope places immutable Evidence ONLY in a delimited untrusted-data field, never in system or developer instructions; strips executable markup and provider tool directives; sends no credentials, verification material, restricted Evidence or field above the classification allowlist; fixes the response to the exact schema and manifest; and rejects unknown or extra fields -- six assertions. TYP-SEC: the output scanner first-match order is `prompt_instruction_leakage`, `secret_or_personal_data`, `prohibited_advisory_domain`, `unsafe_external_action`, `output_schema_invalid`, applied BEFORE Citation validation; any match rejects with `policy_denied` except the already canonical prohibited-advisory and schema reasons, publishes nothing, and permits only a new linked attempt after correction. TYP-OBS: no hidden provider retry exists -- every provider timeout and retry is one of the named AIResponse transitions and appears in the audit trace. TYP-SEC: provider text, prompts, unrestricted responses, credentials and opaque error strings are asserted absent from every event payload and customer output. - rollout: S-10 depends on S-09 for the origin Check Result's impact and confidence metadata and on S-12 for the eligible origin Issue; it gates S-14, which binds a validated AIResponse to a Recommendation Artifact version. Nothing is withheld: the provider gate is settled Volume I behaviour and the complete AIResponse and Citation contract is implemented behind it, so activating an approved signed artifact later requires configuration and its own controlled review rather than new contract work.

### MTX-013 - AC-CAP-013 (CAP: CAP-013)

- Slice: S-11
- Status: Complete; limb withheld
- Withheld limb: OD-032 -- OD-032: canonical namespace for an unassigned record withheld; behaviour permitted under the interim
- contract owner: specification/volume-ii/APPLICATION_LAYER.md#cap-013-evidence-capture-and-provenance
- interface type: Evidence creation is producer-driven and has no command of its own: each producer-enabled `evidence_type` is created by the workflow that observes it. The principal-reachable surface is exactly the Evidence Validation Decision command, plus the read paths the visibility contract governs. CAP-013's Actor is the tenant-scoped integrity-validation service, with a SecurityOperator holding protected `evidence.validation.manage` in authorized security or legal scope.
- route: Not applicable - Volume I defines the actors, the permissions and the command envelope but no HTTP path, method or transport. Transport exposure is owned by API_CONTRACTS.md at the Volume II baseline. There is no Evidence-creation endpoint: creating Evidence out of band would bypass the producer contract that binds each type to its observing workflow.
- request schema: Validation Decision command: Evidence ID, prior effective status, expected validation-state version, resulting status, enumerated reason, policy version, authority, idempotency key, correlation ID. Evidence creation input, per producer: raw observations or immutable references, digest, same-Organization lineage, schema and collector versions, Evidence Classification, validation result, and the Evidence Payload `payload_retention_class`, per CAP-013 Inputs.
- response schema: Valid, invalid or quarantined Evidence with immutable provenance and access history; the appended immutable Evidence Validation Decision; and, when current support ceases to be valid, the atomic unavailable score and suppressed-Recommendation projection, per CAP-013 Outputs.
- controller: Not applicable - no HTTP adapter is defined by Volume I; see route.
- command: `Workflows::Wf007::SubmitEvidenceValidationDecision` (the only principal-reachable path), `Workflows::Wf007::EmitEvidenceRetentionExpiring` (the OD-029 scheduled path under integrity-validation service authority), `Workflows::Wf007::DestroyExpiredEvidencePayload` (the OD-030 path). Evidence creation itself is performed by the producing workflow -- WF-003 for `verification_observation`, WF-005 for `source_document` and `crawl_observation`, WF-006 for `parsed_content` and `external_measurement` -- and CAP-013 contributes the envelope contract those producers MUST satisfy rather than a creation command of its own.
- command input: The Decision command names Evidence, prior effective status, expected validation-state version, resulting status, enumerated reason, policy version, authority, idempotency key and correlation ID. A stale version, a same-status request, or an unlisted transition or reason is rejected WITHOUT a Decision.
- command output: One immutable Evidence Validation Decision containing decision ID, Evidence ID, prior and resulting effective status, reason code, authority, policy version, `decided_at_utc`, state version and correlation ID; plus every applicable propagation effect committed in the same transaction.
- actor: Tenant-scoped integrity-validation service for every allowed transition and reason. A SecurityOperator holding protected `evidence.validation.manage` in active incident, adjudication or support scope may submit ONLY `valid -> quarantined` and only for `security_restriction`, `consent_review` or `retention_review`. Neither may mutate Evidence bytes or an earlier Decision. No other actor may change Evidence validation state.
- organization scope: The Evidence's Organization. Referenced Organization, Project, Source and Evaluation records MUST all belong to it.
- aggregate: Evidence as a lifecycle-bearing auxiliary record with owner Evidence Context and namespace `evd_id`, per 011 DOMAIN_MODEL.md's accepted admission; EvidenceValidationDecision as its immutable append-only history; EvidencePayload as the referenced immutable bytes. Issue, CheckResult, ScoreSnapshot, RecommendationArtifact, Citation and CurrentScoreProjection are propagation targets owned by other slices.
- aggregate boundary: A transition away from `valid` is a deliberately WIDE atomic commit and Volume I says so: before the Decision commits, the same transaction performs every applicable current effect. That is the whole point of the rule -- a narrow commit would leave a window in which a current read returns a numeric score supported by Evidence the system has already decided is not valid.
- value objects: EvidenceId, SchemaVersion, EvidenceType, PayloadReference, ContentSha256, CapturedAtUtc, ObservedAtUtc, SourceSystem, CollectionMethod, CollectorVersion, ValidationStatus, ValidationReasonCode, DataClassification, PayloadRetentionClass, CorrelationId, EvidenceValidationDecisionId, DecidedAtUtc, EvidenceProvenance, EvidenceSource (a conceptual view, never a persisted field).
- domain service: The tenant-scoped integrity-validation service is named by Volume I and is the sole authority for `quarantined -> valid`, for every transition to `invalid`, and for the full reason set on `valid -> quarantined`. It is not a convenience object: its identity is the authority recorded on each Decision.
- repository: `EvidenceRepository`, `EvidenceValidationDecisionRepository`, `EvidencePayloadRepository`, `LegalHoldRepository` read-only at the hold checkpoint, `CurrentScoreProjectionRepository` and `RecommendationArtifactRepository` at the propagation boundary, `CitationRepository` at the propagation boundary.
- persistence model: `evidence` carries every required attribute with `validation_status` as the immutable CREATION-TIME decision; the retained bytes, content digest, tenant references, provenance fields and classification are immutable after creation, so a corrected observation is a NEW Evidence record rather than an update. `evidence_validation_decisions` is append-only and is the authority for effective status; no same-status Decision is created and `invalid` is terminal. Evidence Source is a conceptual origin view over `source_system` and applicable `source_id` and MUST NOT be persisted as a separate field, entity or type literal. `payload_retention_class` classifies ONLY the referenced Evidence Payload; the envelope, digest, provenance, Validation Decisions and downstream lineage are always `product_history`, including after payload destruction.
- migration: Not applicable at this row for Evidence itself - the canonical schema owns `evidence`, `evidence_validation_decisions` and the payload reference, and Evidence's namespace `evd_id` and owner Evidence Context are already admitted by 011 DOMAIN_MODEL.md. WITHHELD under OD-032 and MUST NOT be migrated: a canonical identifier namespace or lifecycle owner for LegalHold or Check Result, or a bounded-context assignment for LegalHold. Volume II is blocked on any artifact that REQUIRES one; behaviour is permitted under the interim and Volume I's existing logical field names remain authoritative for it.
- transaction boundary: One transaction per Decision. It atomically appends the Decision and `EvidenceValidationChanged`, updates every affected current score and Recommendation projection BEFORE commit, and enqueues idempotent recalculation. A transition away from `valid` performs every applicable current effect in that same transaction: if the Evidence supports a selected current Issue or any passed or failed Check Result supplying current pillar coverage, mark the Current Score Projection unavailable, add `invalid_evidence` to its de-duplicated fixed-precedence reason set, and suppress every published Recommendation Artifact whose origin Issue is affected; INDEPENDENTLY, suppress every published Artifact whose rationale or Citation references that Evidence. The two suppression limbs are separate predicates and neither subsumes the other.
- concurrency: The expected validation-state version is mandatory: a stale command is rejected without a Decision, so two operators cannot both transition one Evidence record. `validation_status` is immutable at creation and effective status derives from the append-only Decision history, so a concurrent read never observes a half-applied transition. Check Results and historical snapshots retain the Validation Decision IDs and statuses frozen when they were created and never mutate, so a Decision landing concurrently with a Check persist cannot rewrite the frozen tuple. Recalculation is idempotent and is enqueued rather than run inline, so a redelivery cannot double-apply.
- idempotency: The Decision command is idempotent by its idempotency key: exact replay returns the Decision WITHOUT repeating suppression, the event, or recalculation. A same-status request creates no Decision at all rather than a duplicate. `EvidenceRetentionExpiring` is emitted EXACTLY ONCE per Evidence Payload at its 30-day boundary; the emitted cursor is retained so a rescheduled evaluation emits no second event. Payload destruction is idempotent on the payload reference: a second destruction of already-destroyed bytes appends no second `security_audit` record.
- background job: `EvidenceRetentionExpiringJob` at 30 days before the current `product_evidence_payload` retention maximum; `EvidencePayloadDestructionJob` at the 24-month elapsed maximum on the capture cursor; `EvidenceRecalculationJob` for the idempotent score and Recommendation recalculation each Decision enqueues. No job may mutate Evidence bytes, an earlier Decision, or a historical snapshot.
- queue: The evaluation and lifecycle queues, per the Volume I Background Processing action registry. Queue identity is owned by BACKGROUND_PROCESSING.md; this row does not rename it.
- retry policy: Not applicable as a workflow retry - CAP-013 defines none. A rejected Decision is terminal for that command: a stale version, same-status request, or unlisted transition or reason is refused and the correct recovery is a new Decision against the LATEST version, not a retry of the refused one. The recalculation the Decision enqueues is idempotent, so its own redelivery repeats no effect.
- terminal failure: `invalid` is terminal: no transition leaves it, and corrected content is a new Evidence record. Creation or downstream use fails for an invalid field, missing bytes, digest mismatch, unknown schema, tenant mismatch or an unknown `payload_retention_class`, and creates no record on rejection. Reserved `operator_attestation` returns `F1-DOMAIN-409 / evidence_type_unavailable` on every attempted creation; an unknown or legacy alias, including `operator_submission`, returns `F1-VALIDATION-400 / evidence_type_invalid`. No implementation may normalize an alias into a recognized type.
- reconciliation: A later `quarantined -> valid` triggers recalculation, removes `invalid_evidence` ONLY when no selected score input has invalid Evidence, and may restore a promoted snapshot and eligible Recommendation Artifacts. No automatic republication occurs until every publication predicate passes again. Recalculation replaces the COMPLETE reason set with all and only reasons applicable to the recalculated inputs -- it does not subtract one code and leave the rest. A legal hold suspends destruction and the payload is destroyed at the first eligible instant after release.
- domain events: `EvidenceValidationChanged`, emitted in the same transaction as every accepted transition. `EvidenceRetentionExpiring`, which under OD-029 Option 2 has exactly one producer -- WF-007 under the integrity-validation service authority -- and is emitted once per Evidence Payload at 30 days before its current `product_evidence_payload` retention maximum. Under OD-030 Option 3 payload destruction emits NO domain event and appends NO Evidence Validation Decision: it is proved by exactly one immutable `security_audit` deletion audit record.
- event payload: `EvidenceValidationChanged`: the canonical envelope with the real `organization_id`, plus Decision ID, Evidence ID, prior and resulting effective status, reason code, authority, policy version, `decided_at_utc`, state version and correlation ID. `EvidenceRetentionExpiring`: the Evidence identity, its Organization, the capture cursor, the maximum instant and the correlation ID -- exactly those fields. Neither payload carries Evidence bytes, challenge plaintext or restricted observation content.
- event producer: `Workflows::Wf007` is the sole producer of both events. OD-029 makes that exclusive for `EvidenceRetentionExpiring`: no lifecycle service, no second workflow and no notification route variant produces it.
- event consumers: S-13 consumes `EvidenceValidationChanged` as the trigger for idempotent score recalculation; S-14 consumes it for Recommendation suppression and republication. WF-011 consumes the `EvidenceRetentionExpiring` reassessment request through the accepted path when the Project remains active. No consumer may re-decide the Decision.
- serializer: Not applicable - no transport exposure is defined by Volume I; see route.
- authorization entry point: The Decision command, before mutation, resolving the exact actor limits: the integrity-validation service for every allowed transition and reason; a SecurityOperator only for `valid -> quarantined` and only for the three permitted reasons, and only in active incident, adjudication or support scope.
- permission checks: `evidence.validation.manage`, whose Permission Baseline cell reads `quarantine-only in authorized security/legal scope` for a SecurityOperator and `integrity-validation service only for all allowed transitions` for the service, and deny for every other role. It is a PROTECTED grant, so it requires approval within 24 hours by a SecurityOperator other than the requester and an active expiry. `evidence.restricted.read` is a separate protected explicit grant that raises ONLY the granted OrganizationAdmin's Evidence payload access to `restricted`; it broadens no resource scope and no other field permission, and it authorizes no validation transition.
- tenant boundary: Validation Rule 1 is absolute: referenced Organization, Project, Source and Evaluation records MUST belong to the same Organization. A cross-Organization reference, missing referenced bytes, digest mismatch or unknown schema version MUST reject the downstream write with a data-integrity error AND an audit event. A Support Session scopes an already-permitted action to an Organization, resource and action allowlist; it is not itself a read grant and never widens a Permission Baseline cell.
- error contract: `F1-DOMAIN-409 / evidence_type_unavailable` for reserved `operator_attestation`. `F1-VALIDATION-400 / evidence_type_invalid` for an unknown or legacy alias including `operator_submission`. Rejection without a Decision for a stale expected validation-state version, a same-status request, or an unlisted transition or reason. Creation rejection for an invalid field, missing immutable bytes, digest mismatch, unknown schema version, tenant mismatch or an unknown `payload_retention_class`. Downstream data-integrity rejection with an audit event for a cross-Organization reference, missing referenced bytes, digest mismatch or unknown schema version. Every rejection creates no record.
